#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

extern char *__realpath_chk (const char *__restrict __name,
			     char *__restrict __resolved,
			     size_t __resolvedlen) __THROW __wur;
extern char *__REDIRECT_NTH (__realpath_alias,
			     (const char *__restrict __name,
			      char *__restrict __resolved), realpath) __wur;
extern char *__REDIRECT_NTH (__realpath_chk_warn,
			     (const char *__restrict __name,
			      char *__restrict __resolved,
			      size_t __resolvedlen), __realpath_chk) __wur
     __warnattr ("second argument of realpath must be either NULL or at "
		 "least PATH_MAX bytes long buffer");

__fortify_function __wur char *
__NTH (realpath (const char *__restrict __name, char *__restrict __resolved))
{
  size_t sz = __glibc_objsize (__resolved);

  if (sz == (size_t) -1)
    return __realpath_alias (__name, __resolved);

#if defined _LIBC_LIMITS_H_ && defined PATH_MAX
  if (__glibc_unsafe_len (PATH_MAX, sizeof (char), sz))
    return __realpath_chk_warn (__name, __resolved, sz);
#endif
  return __realpath_chk (__name, __resolved, sz);
}


extern int __ptsname_r_chk (int __fd, char *__buf, size_t __buflen,
			    size_t __nreal) __THROW __nonnull ((2))
    __attr_access ((__write_only__, 2, 3));
extern int __REDIRECT_NTH (__ptsname_r_alias, (int __fd, char *__buf,

     __nonnull ((2)) __attr_access ((__write_only__, 2, 3));

__fortify_function int
__NTH (ptsname_r (int __fd, char *__buf, size_t __buflen))
{
  return __glibc_fortify (ptsname_r, __buflen, sizeof (char),
			  __glibc_objsize (__buf),
			  __fd, __buf, __buflen);
}


extern int __wctomb_chk (char *__s, wchar_t __wchar, size_t __buflen)
  __THROW __wur;
extern int __REDIRECT_NTH (__wctomb_alias, (char *__s, wchar_t __wchar),
			   wctomb) __wur;

__fortify_function __wur int
__NTH (wctomb (char *__s, wchar_t __wchar))
{
  /* We would have to include <limits.h> to get a definition of MB_LEN_MAX.
     But this would only disturb the namespace.  So we define our own
     version here.  */
#define __STDLIB_MB_LEN_MAX	16
#if defined MB_LEN_MAX && MB_LEN_MAX != __STDLIB_MB_LEN_MAX
# error "Assumed value of MB_LEN_MAX wrong"
#endif
  if (__glibc_objsize (__s) != (size_t) -1
      && __STDLIB_MB_LEN_MAX > __glibc_objsize (__s))
    return __wctomb_chk (__s, __wchar, __glibc_objsize (__s));
  return __wctomb_alias (__s, __wchar);
}


extern size_t __mbstowcs_chk (wchar_t *__restrict __dst,
			      const char *__restrict __src,
			      size_t __len, size_t __dstlen) __THROW
    __attr_access ((__write_only__, 1, 3)) __attr_access ((__read_only__, 2));
extern size_t __REDIRECT_NTH (__mbstowcs_nulldst,
			      (wchar_t *__restrict __dst,
			       const char *__restrict __src,
			       size_t __len), mbstowcs)
    __attr_access ((__read_only__, 2));
extern size_t __REDIRECT_NTH (__mbstowcs_alias,
			      (wchar_t *__restrict __dst,
			       const char *__restrict __src,
			       size_t __len), mbstowcs)
    __attr_access ((__write_only__, 1, 3)) __attr_access ((__read_only__, 2));
extern size_t __REDIRECT_NTH (__mbstowcs_chk_warn,
			      (wchar_t *__restrict __dst,
			       const char *__restrict __src,
			       size_t __len, size_t __dstlen), __mbstowcs_chk)
     __warnattr ("mbstowcs called with dst buffer smaller than len "
		 "* sizeof (wchar_t)");

__fortify_function size_t
__NTH (mbstowcs (wchar_t *__restrict __dst, const char *__restrict __src,
		 size_t __len))
{

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <cerrno>
#include <climits>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <mpi.h>
#include <omp.h>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
constexpr int TILE = 32;
constexpr int TILE_CELLS = TILE * TILE;
constexpr int BLOCK_ROWS = 8;
constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE / BLOCK_ROWS;

// During computation, via is one plus the smallest possible largest internal
// vertex on a shortest path (zero denotes a direct edge). Minimizing this on
// equal distances preserves the original ascending-k, strict-update path
// matrix even though blocked Floyd-Warshall changes the evaluation order.
struct alignas(8) Cell {
    unsigned int distance;
    unsigned int via;
};
static_assert(sizeof(Cell) == 2 * sizeof(unsigned int));
int worldRank = -1;

void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* expression) {
    if (error "'!= cudaSuccess) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s: %s'"\\n\", worldRank, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        std::abort();
    }
}
void mpiCheck(int error, const char* expression) {
    if (error "'!= MPI_SUCCESS) {
        char message[MPI_MAX_ERROR_STRING];
        int length = 0;
        MPI_Error_string(error, message, &length);
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s: %.*s'"\\n\", worldRank, expression, length, message);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        std::abort();
    }
}
#define CUDA_CHECK(call) cudaCheck((call), #call)
#define MPI_CHECK(call) mpiCheck((call), #call)

struct Grid {
    MPI_Comm cart = MPI_COMM_NULL, row = MPI_COMM_NULL, column = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    int size, rank, dims[2] = {0, 0}, coords[2], rows, columns;
    size_t elements;
    explicit Grid(int tiles) {
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size));
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims));
        int periodic[2] = {0, 0};
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dims, periodic, 0, &cart));
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(cart, &rank));
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Cart_coords(cart, rank, 2, coords));
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split(cart, coords[0], coords[1], &row));
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split(cart, coords[1], coords[0], &column));
        rows = tiles > coords[0] ? (tiles - 1 - coords[0]) / dims[0] + 1 : 0;
        columns = tiles > coords[1] ? (tiles - 1 - coords[1]) / dims[1] + 1 : 0;
        elements = size_t(rows) * columns * TILE_CELLS;
    }
    ~Grid() {
        MPI_Comm_free(&row);
        MPI_Comm_free(&column);
        MPI_Comm_free(&cart);
    }
    Grid(const Grid&) = delete;
    Grid& operator=(const Grid&) = delete;
};

struct DeviceCells {
    Cell* data = nullptr;
    explicit DeviceCells(size_t count) {
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data),
                              std::max(count, size_t(1)) * sizeof(Cell)));
    }
    ~DeviceCells() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceCells(const DeviceCells&) = delete;
    DeviceCells& operator=(const DeviceCells&) = delete;
};
struct PinnedCells {
    Cell* data = nullptr;
    explicit PinnedCells(size_t count) {
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data),
                                 std::max(count, size_t(1)) * sizeof(Cell), cudaHostAllocPortable));
    }
    ~PinnedCells() { cudaFreeHost(data); }
    PinnedCells(const PinnedCells&) = delete;
    PinnedCells& operator=(const PinnedCells&) = delete;
};
int hostThreads(size_t work) {
    return std::min(omp_get_max_threads(), int(std::min(size_t(16), work / 32768 + 1)));
}

// glibc rand_r advances a 32-bit LCG three times per output. Exponentiating
// this affine map gives each tile row its exact position in the original
// random stream without generating or replicating the global matrix.
unsigned int randomSeedAt(size_t offset) {
    uint32_t multiplier = 1, increment = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        multiplier *= 1103515245u;
        increment = increment * 1103515245u + 12345u;
    }
    uint32_t seed = 42;
    while (offset) {
        if (offset & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
        increment *= multiplier + 1;
        multiplier *= multiplier;
        offset >>= 1;
    }
    return seed;
}

void initializeMatrix(std::vector<Cell>& local, size_t n, const Grid& grid) {
#if "'!defined(__GLIBC__)
    // Preserve the platform'"'s rand_r sequence on other C libraries as well.
    std::vector<unsigned int> rowSeeds(n);
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        unsigned int seed = 42;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            rowSeeds[i] = seed;
            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) (void)rand_r(&seed);
        }
    }
    for (size_t start = 0; start < n;) {
        const int count = int(std::min(n - start, size_t(INT_MAX)));
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(rowSeeds.data() + start, count, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, grid.cart));
        start += count;
    }
#endif
    const size_t tileRows = size_t(grid.rows) * grid.columns * TILE;
    const int threads = hostThreads(local.size());
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(threads)
    for (size_t line = 0; line < tileRows; ++line) {
        const size_t tile = line / TILE;
        const size_t row = size_t(grid.coords[0] + int(tile / grid.columns) * grid.dims[0])
                           * TILE + line % TILE;
        const size_t firstColumn = size_t(grid.coords[1] + int(tile % grid.columns)
                                         * grid.dims[1]) * TILE;
        unsigned int seed = 42;
        if (row < n && firstColumn < n) {
#if defined(__GLIBC__)
            seed = randomSeedAt(row * n + firstColumn);
#else
            seed = rowSeeds[row];
            for (size_t j = 0; j < firstColumn; ++j) (void)rand_r(&seed);
#endif
        }
        for (int j = 0; j < TILE; ++j) {
            const size_t column = firstColumn + j;
            unsigned int distance = INF;
            if (row < n && column < n)
                distance = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(
                    double(MAX_DISTANCE) * rand_r(&seed) / double(RAND_MAX));
            if (row == column) distance = 0;
            local[tile * TILE_CELLS + (line % TILE) * TILE + j] = {distance, 0};
        }
    }
}

__device__ __forceinline__ bool relax(Cell& current, Cell left, Cell right,
                                     unsigned int vertex) {
    const unsigned int distance = left.distance + right.distance;
    const unsigned int via = max(vertex, max(left.via, right.via));
    if (distance < current.distance || (distance == current.distance && via < current.via)) {
        current = {distance, via};
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

__global__ void updateDiagonal(Cell* diagonal, unsigned int firstVertex) {
    __shared__ Cell tile[TILE][TILE];
    const int column = threadIdx.x, firstRow = threadIdx.y;
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        tile[row][column] = diagonal[row * TILE + column];
    }
    __syncthreads();
    #pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            // Zero diagonals and positive edges make pivot rows/columns
            // invariant. Leaving them untouched avoids shared-memory races.
            if (row "'!= k && column != k) {
                Cell value = tile[row][column];
                if (relax(value, tile[row][k], tile[k][column], firstVertex + k + 1))
                    tile[row][column] = value;
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        diagonal[row * TILE + column] = tile[row][column];
    }
}

__global__ void updatePanels(Cell* matrix, const Cell* diagonal, int rows, int columns,
                             int pivotRow, int pivotColumn, bool ownsRow, bool ownsColumn,
                             unsigned int firstVertex) {
    const bool rowPanel = blockIdx.y == 0;
    const int index = blockIdx.x;
    if (rowPanel ? (!ownsRow || index >= columns || index == pivotColumn)
                 : (!ownsColumn || index >= rows || index == pivotRow)) return;
    const size_t tileIndex = rowPanel ? size_t(pivotRow) * columns + index
                                     : size_t(index) * columns + pivotColumn;
    Cell* output = matrix + tileIndex * TILE_CELLS;
    __shared__ Cell pivot[TILE][TILE];
    __shared__ Cell tile[TILE][TILE];
    const int column = threadIdx.x, firstRow = threadIdx.y;
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        pivot[row][column] = diagonal[row * TILE + column];
        tile[row][column] = output[row * TILE + column];
    }
    __syncthreads();
    #pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            if (rowPanel ? row != k : column != k) {
                Cell value = tile[row][column];
                const Cell left = rowPanel ? pivot[row][k] : tile[row][k];
                const Cell right = rowPanel ? tile[k][column] : pivot[k][column];
                if (relax(value, left, right, firstVertex + k + 1)) tile[row][column] = value;
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        output[row * TILE + column] = tile[row][column];
    }
}

__global__ void packColumn(const Cell* matrix, Cell* panel, size_t count,
                           int columns, int pivotColumn) {
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;
         i += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x)
        panel[i] = matrix[(i / TILE_CELLS * columns + pivotColumn) * TILE_CELLS + i % TILE_CELLS];
}

__global__ void updateRemaining(Cell* matrix, const Cell* columnPanel, const Cell* rowPanel,
                                size_t tiles, int columns, int pivotRow, int pivotColumn,
                                bool ownsRow, bool ownsColumn, unsigned int firstVertex) {
    __shared__ Cell left[TILE][TILE];
    __shared__ Cell right[TILE][TILE];
    const int column = threadIdx.x, firstRow = threadIdx.y;
    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tiles; tile += gridDim.x) {
        const int tileRow = int(tile / columns), tileColumn = int(tile % columns);
        if ((ownsRow && tileRow == pivotRow) || (ownsColumn && tileColumn == pivotColumn)) continue;
        Cell values[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            const int element = row * TILE + column;
            left[row][column] = columnPanel[size_t(tileRow) * TILE_CELLS + element];
            right[row][column] = rowPanel[size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_CELLS + element];
            values[r] = matrix[tile * TILE_CELLS + element];
        }
        __syncthreads();
        #pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
            const Cell b = right[k][column];
            #pragma unroll
            for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r)
                relax(values[r], left[firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS][k], b, firstVertex + k + 1);
        }
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            matrix[tile * TILE_CELLS + row * TILE + column] = values[r];
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
}

__global__ void restorePathIndices(Cell* matrix, size_t count, int columns,
                                   int columnCoordinate, int columnRanks) {
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;
         i += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const unsigned int column = unsigned(columnCoordinate
            + int(i / TILE_CELLS % columns) * columnRanks) * TILE + i % TILE;
        matrix[i].via = matrix[i].via ? matrix[i].via - 1 : column;
    }
}
unsigned int blocksFor(size_t elements) {
    return unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (elements + 255) / 256));
}

// Host staging supports ordinary MPI without requiring CUDA-aware MPI.
// Chunk collectives to support matrices larger than MPI'"'s int count.
void broadcastAsync(Cell* cells, size_t count, int root, MPI_Comm communicator,
                    std::vector<MPI_Request>& requests) {
    auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(cells);
    size_t remaining = count * sizeof(Cell);
    do {
        const int chunk = int(std::min(remaining, size_t(INT_MAX)));
        MPI_Request request;
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Ibcast(bytes, chunk, MPI_BYTE, root, communicator, &request));
        requests.push_back(request);
        bytes += chunk;
        remaining -= chunk;
    } while (remaining);
}
void waitBroadcasts(std::vector<MPI_Request>& requests) {
    if ("'!requests.empty()) {
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(int(requests.size()), requests.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
        requests.clear();
    }
}

void floydWarshall(Cell* matrix, int tiles, const Grid& grid) {
    const size_t rowCount = size_t(grid.columns) * TILE_CELLS;
    const size_t columnCount = size_t(grid.rows) * TILE_CELLS;
    DeviceCells diagonal(TILE_CELLS), rowPanel(rowCount), columnPanel(columnCount);
    PinnedCells hostDiagonal(TILE_CELLS), hostRow(rowCount), hostColumn(columnCount);
    cudaStream_t stream;
    cudaEvent_t copied;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&copied, cudaEventDisableTiming));
    const dim3 threads(TILE, BLOCK_ROWS);
    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
    requests.reserve(4);
    for (int k = 0; k < tiles; ++k) {
        // Wait only for previous host-to-device copies; the preceding interior
        // update can continue while the CPU progresses communication.
        if (k && grid.size > 1) CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(copied));
        const int ownerRow = k % grid.dims[0], ownerColumn = k % grid.dims[1];
        const bool ownsRow = grid.coords[0] == ownerRow;
        const bool ownsColumn = grid.coords[1] == ownerColumn;
        const bool ownsDiagonal = ownsRow && ownsColumn;
        const int pivotRow = k / grid.dims[0], pivotColumn = k / grid.dims[1];
        Cell* localDiagonal = matrix;
        if (ownsDiagonal) {
            localDiagonal += (size_t(pivotRow) * grid.columns + pivotColumn) * TILE_CELLS;
            updateDiagonal<<<1, threads, 0, stream>>>(localDiagonal, k * TILE);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
            if (grid.size > 1) {
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostDiagonal.data, localDiagonal,
                    TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
            }
        }
        if (ownsRow && grid.dims[1] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(hostDiagonal.data, TILE_CELLS, ownerColumn, grid.row, requests);
        if (ownsColumn && grid.dims[0] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(hostDiagonal.data, TILE_CELLS, ownerRow, grid.column, requests);
        waitBroadcasts(requests);
        if ((ownsRow || ownsColumn) && !ownsDiagonal)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(diagonal.data, hostDiagonal.data,
                TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
        if ((ownsRow || ownsColumn) && std::max(grid.rows, grid.columns)) {
            updatePanels<<<dim3(std::max(grid.rows, grid.columns), 2), threads, 0, stream>>>(
                matrix, ownsDiagonal ? localDiagonal : diagonal.data, grid.rows, grid.columns,
                pivotRow, pivotColumn, ownsRow, ownsColumn, k * TILE);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
        }
        const Cell* localRow = rowPanel.data;
        if (ownsRow) localRow = matrix + size_t(pivotRow) * grid.columns * TILE_CELLS;
        if (ownsColumn && columnCount) {
            packColumn<<<blocksFor(columnCount), 256, 0, stream>>>(
                matrix, columnPanel.data, columnCount, grid.columns, pivotColumn);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
        }
        bool staged = false;
        if (ownsRow && grid.dims[0] > 1 && rowCount) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostRow.data, localRow, rowCount * sizeof(Cell),
                cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            staged = true;
        }
        if (ownsColumn && grid.dims[1] > 1 && columnCount) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostColumn.data, columnPanel.data,
                columnCount * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            staged = true;
        }
        if (staged) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        if (grid.dims[0] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(hostRow.data, rowCount, ownerRow, grid.column, requests);
        if (grid.dims[1] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(hostColumn.data, columnCount, ownerColumn, grid.row, requests);
        waitBroadcasts(requests);
        if (!ownsRow && rowCount)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rowPanel.data, hostRow.data, rowCount * sizeof(Cell),
                cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
        if (!ownsColumn && columnCount)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(columnPanel.data, hostColumn.data,
                columnCount * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
        if (grid.size > 1) CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(copied, stream));
        const size_t localTiles = size_t(grid.rows) * grid.columns;
        if (grid.rows > int(ownsRow) && grid.columns > int(ownsColumn)) {
            updateRemaining<<<unsigned(std::min(localTiles, size_t(65535))), threads, 0, stream>>>(
                matrix, columnPanel.data, localRow, localTiles, grid.columns,
                pivotRow, pivotColumn, ownsRow, ownsColumn, k * TILE);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
        }
    }
    if (grid.elements) {
        restorePathIndices<<<blocksFor(grid.elements), 256, 0, stream>>>(
            matrix, grid.elements, grid.columns, grid.coords[1], grid.dims[1]);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(copied));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
}

void gatherResults(const std::vector<Cell>& local, size_t n, int tiles, const Grid& grid,
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>* path = nullptr) {
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        dist.resize(n * n);
        if (path) path->resize(n * n);
    }
    std::vector<Cell> received;
    for (int rank = 0; rank < grid.size; ++rank) {
        int coordinates[2];
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Cart_coords(grid.cart, rank, 2, coordinates));
        const int rows = tiles > coordinates[0] ? (tiles - 1 - coordinates[0]) / grid.dims[0] + 1 : 0;
        const int columns = tiles > coordinates[1] ? (tiles - 1 - coordinates[1]) / grid.dims[1] + 1 : 0;
        const size_t count = size_t(rows) * columns * TILE_CELLS;
        if (grid.rank == 0 && rank) received.resize(count);
        if (rank && (grid.rank == 0 || grid.rank == rank)) {
            auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(grid.rank == 0 ? received.data()
                           : const_cast<Cell*>(local.data()));
            for (size_t remaining = count * sizeof(Cell); remaining;) {
                const int chunk = int(std::min(remaining, size_t(INT_MAX)));
                if (grid.rank == 0)
                    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(bytes, chunk, MPI_BYTE, rank, 0, grid.cart, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));
                else
                    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(bytes, chunk, MPI_BYTE, 0, 0, grid.cart));
                bytes += chunk;
                remaining -= chunk;
            }
        }
        if (grid.rank != 0) continue;
        const Cell* data = rank ? received.data() : local.data();
        const int threads = hostThreads(count);
        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(threads)
        for (size_t line = 0; line < size_t(rows) * columns * TILE; ++line) {
            const size_t tile = line / TILE;
            const size_t row = size_t(coordinates[0] + int(tile / columns) * grid.dims[0])
                               * TILE + line % TILE;
            const size_t firstColumn = size_t(coordinates[1] + int(tile % columns) * grid.dims[1]) * TILE;
            if (row >= n) continue;
            for (int j = 0; j < TILE && firstColumn + j < n; ++j) {
                const Cell value = data[tile * TILE_CELLS + (line % TILE) * TILE + j];
                dist[row * n + firstColumn + j] = value.distance;
                if (path) (*path)[row * n + firstColumn + j] = value.via;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, size_t n) {
    int invalid = 0;
    const int threads = hostThreads(n * n);
    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(|:invalid) schedule(static) num_threads(threads)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) invalid |= dist[i * n + i] != 0;
    const size_t sample = std::min(n, size_t(10));
    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(|:invalid) schedule(static) num_threads(threads)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sample; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < sample; ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                const unsigned int a = dist[i * n + k], b = dist[k * n + j];
                if (a < INF && b < INF) invalid |= a + b < dist[i * n + j];
            }
        }
    }
    return !invalid;
}

void printUsage(const char* name) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]'"\\n\", name);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
    printf(\"Launch one MPI rank per GPU; OMP_NUM_THREADS controls host parallelism.\\n\");
}

int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
    MPI_Comm node;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
    int localRank, devices = 0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
    if ("'!devices) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.'"\\n\", worldRank);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
    const int tiles = int((n + TILE - 1) / TILE);
    Grid grid(tiles);
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");
        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", n);
        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
        printf(\"MPI grid: %d x %d; CUDA tile: %d x %d\\n\", grid.dims[0], grid.dims[1], TILE, TILE);
        printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");
    }
    std::vector<Cell> local(grid.elements);
    initializeMatrix(local, n, grid);
    DeviceCells matrix(grid.elements);
    if (grid.elements)
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(matrix.data, local.data(), grid.elements * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    if (grid.rank == 0) printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(grid.cart));
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    floydWarshall(matrix.data, tiles, grid);
    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double seconds = 0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, grid.cart));
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        printf(\"Computation time: %lld ms\\n\", static_cast<long long>(seconds * 1000));
        const double operations = double(n) * double(n) * double(n);
        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", seconds > 0 ? operations / seconds / 1e9 : 0.0);
    }
    int status = 0;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        if (grid.elements)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(local.data(), matrix.data, grid.elements * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        std::vector<unsigned int> dist;
        gatherResults(local, n, tiles, grid, dist);
        if (grid.rank == 0) {
            if (printResults) print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");
            if (validate) {
                printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");
                status = validateResult(dist, n) ? 0 : 1;
                printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", status ? \"FAILED\" : \"PASSED\");
            }
        }
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, grid.cart));
    return status;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int provided = 0;
    if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided) "'!= MPI_SUCCESS) return 1;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
    if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
        if (worldRank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED support is required.'"\\n\");
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false, printResults = false;
    int status = 0;
    bool run = true;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            char* end = nullptr;
            errno = 0;
            const char* argument = argv[++i];
            const unsigned long long value = strtoull(argument, &end, 10);
            if (errno || argument[0] == '-' || end == argument || *end ||
                value > unsigned(INT_MAX - TILE) ||
                (value && value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Cell) / value)) {
                if (worldRank == 0) fprintf(stderr, \"Invalid number of nodes: %s\\n\", argument);
                status = 1;
                run = false;
                break;
            }
            n = size_t(value);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            run = false;
            break;
        } else {
            if (worldRank == 0) {
                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            status = 1;
            run = false;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (run) {
        try {
            status = runBenchmark(n, validate, printResults);
        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
            fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: %s\\n\", worldRank, error.what());
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
    return status;
}

import os, subprocess
src = \"#define main hybrid_main\\n#include \\\"/home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/floydwarshall_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_hybrid_r3/floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp\\\"\\n#undef main\\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\\n    int provided;\\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);\\n    MPI_Comm node;\\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node);\\n    int localRank, devices;\\n    MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank); MPI_Comm_free(&node);\\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));\\n    for (size_t n : {size_t(0),size_t(1),size_t(2),size_t(7),size_t(31),size_t(32),size_t(33),\\n                     size_t(63),size_t(64),size_t(65),size_t(97),size_t(127),size_t(128),\\n                     size_t(129),size_t(191),size_t(257),size_t(513)}) {\\n        const int tiles = (n + TILE - 1) / TILE;\\n        Grid grid(tiles);\\n        std::vector<Cell> local(grid.elements);\\n        initializeMatrix(local, n, grid);\\n        std::vector<unsigned> input, expected, expectedPath, actual, actualPath;\\n        gatherResults(local,n,tiles,grid,input);\\n        if ("'!worldRank) {'"\\n            unsigned seed = 42; expected.resize(n*n); expectedPath.resize(n*n);\\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n*n; ++i) {\\n                expected[i] = 1 + (unsigned)(double(MAX_DISTANCE)*rand_r(&seed)/double(RAND_MAX));\\n                expectedPath[i] = i % n;\\n            }\\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) expected[i*n+i]=0;\\n            if (input "'!= expected) {'"\\n                fprintf(stderr, \\\"INITIALIZATION MISMATCH n=%zu\\\\n\\\",n); MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD,2);\\n            }\\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k)\\n                for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i)\\n                    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\\n                        unsigned candidate = expected[i*n+k]+expected[k*n+j];\\n                        if (candidate < expected[i*n+j]) {\\n                            expected[i*n+j]=candidate; expectedPath[i*n+j]=k;\\n                        }\\n                    }\\n        }\\n        DeviceCells device(grid.elements);\\n        if (grid.elements) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(device.data,local.data(),grid.elements*sizeof(Cell),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\\n        floydWarshall(device.data,tiles,grid);\\n        if (grid.elements) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(local.data(),device.data,grid.elements*sizeof(Cell),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\\n        gatherResults(local,n,tiles,grid,actual,&actualPath);\\n        if ("'!worldRank) {'"\\n            for (size_t i=0;i<n*n;++i)\\n                if (actual[i]"'!=expected[i] || actualPath[i]!=expectedPath[i]) {'"\\n                    fprintf(stderr,\\\"MISMATCH n=%zu [%zu,%zu] dist=%u expected=%u path=%u expected=%u\\\\n\\\",\\n                            n,i/n,i%n,actual[i],expected[i],actualPath[i],expectedPath[i]);\\n                    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD,3);\\n                }\\n            printf(\\\"Exact distance and path match: n=%zu\\\\n\\\",n); fflush(stdout);\\n        }\\n    }\\n    MPI_Finalize(); return 0;\\n}\\n\"
fd=os.memfd_create('floydwarshall-test')
os.write(fd,src.encode())
subprocess.run(['nvcc','-x','cu','/proc/'+str(os.getpid())+'/fd/'+str(fd),'-std=c++17','-O3','-arch=native','-Xcompiler=-fopenmp','-DOMPI_SKIP_MPICXX=1','-DMPICH_SKIP_MPICXX=1','-I/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/openmpi/include','-lmpi','-lgomp','-o',\"/tmp/floydwarshall-hybrid-YEHOxH/check\"],check=True)

import os,subprocess
src=open('floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp').read().replace('constexpr int TILE = 32;','constexpr int TILE = 64;')
src=src.replace('__shared__ Cell pivot[TILE][TILE];\\n    __shared__ Cell tile[TILE][TILE];','extern __shared__ Cell shared[];\\n    Cell (*pivot)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared);\\n    Cell (*tile)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared + TILE_CELLS);')
src=src.replace('__shared__ Cell left[TILE][TILE];\\n    __shared__ Cell right[TILE][TILE];','extern __shared__ Cell shared[];\\n    Cell (*left)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared);\\n    Cell (*right)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared + TILE_CELLS);')
src=src.replace('updatePanels<<<dim3(std::max(grid.rows, grid.columns), 2), threads, 0, stream>>>','updatePanels<<<dim3(std::max(grid.rows, grid.columns), 2), threads, 2*TILE_CELLS*sizeof(Cell), stream>>>')
src=src.replace('updateRemaining<<<unsigned(std::min(localTiles, size_t(65535))), threads, 0, stream>>>','updateRemaining<<<unsigned(std::min(localTiles, size_t(65535))), threads, 2*TILE_CELLS*sizeof(Cell), stream>>>')
src=src.replace('cudaFuncAttributes attributes;','CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(updatePanels,cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,2*TILE_CELLS*sizeof(Cell)));\\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(updateRemaining,cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,2*TILE_CELLS*sizeof(Cell)));\\n    cudaFuncAttributes attributes;')
fd=os.memfd_create('floydwarshall-64')
os.write(fd,src.encode())

+bool deviceMPIAvailable(const Grid& grid) {
+    int available = 0;
+#if defined(OMPI_HAVE_MPI_EXT_CUDA) && OMPI_HAVE_MPI_EXT_CUDA
+    available = MPIX_Query_cuda_support();
+#endif
+    int supported = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&available, &supported, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, grid.cart));
+    return supported != 0;
+}

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
p='floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('constexpr int TILE = 32;\\nconstexpr int TILE_CELLS = TILE * TILE;\\n','')
s=s.replace('constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE / BLOCK_ROWS;\\n','')
s=s.replace('explicit Grid(int tiles) {','explicit Grid(int tiles, int tileWidth = 32) {')
s=s.replace('elements = size_t(rows) * columns * TILE_CELLS;','elements = size_t(rows) * columns * tileWidth * tileWidth;')
for name in ['initializeMatrix','updateDiagonal','updatePanels','packColumn','updateRemaining','restorePathIndices','floydWarshall','gatherResults']:
    signatures={'initializeMatrix':'void initializeMatrix(', 'updateDiagonal':'__global__ void updateDiagonal(', 'updatePanels':'__global__ void updatePanels(', 'packColumn':'__global__ void packColumn(', 'updateRemaining':'__global__ void updateRemaining(', 'restorePathIndices':'__global__ void restorePathIndices(', 'floydWarshall':'double floydWarshall(', 'gatherResults':'void gatherResults('}
    start=s.index(signatures[name])
    brace=s.index('{',start)
    constants='\\n    constexpr int TILE = B;\\n    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;'
    if name in ['updateDiagonal','updatePanels','updateRemaining']:
        constants+='\\n    constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = B / BLOCK_ROWS;'
    if name == 'restorePathIndices': constants='\\n    constexpr int TILE = B;\\n    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;'
    s=s[:brace+1]+constants+s[brace+1:]
    s=s[:start]+'template<int B>\\n'+s[start:]
for name in ['updateDiagonal','updatePanels','packColumn','updateRemaining','restorePathIndices']:
    s=s.replace(name+'<<<',name+'<B><<<')
    s=s.replace('&attributes, '+name+')','&attributes, '+name+'<B>)')
s=s.replace('__shared__ Cell pivot[TILE][TILE];\\n    __shared__ Cell tile[TILE][TILE];','extern __shared__ Cell shared[];\\n    Cell (*pivot)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared);\\n    Cell (*tile)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared + TILE_CELLS);')
s=s.replace('__shared__ Cell left[TILE][TILE];\\n    __shared__ Cell right[TILE][TILE];','extern __shared__ Cell shared[];\\n    Cell (*left)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared);\\n    Cell (*right)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared + TILE_CELLS);')
s=s.replace('threads, 0, stream>>>(\\n                matrix, pivotData','threads, 2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), stream>>>(\\n                matrix, pivotData')
s=s.replace('threads, 0, stream>>>(\\n                matrix, columnData','threads, 2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), stream>>>(\\n                matrix, columnData')
s=s.replace('cudaFuncAttributes attributes;','if constexpr (B > 32) {\\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(updatePanels<B>, cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\\n                                        2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell)));\\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(updateRemaining<B>, cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\\n                                        2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell)));\\n    }\\n    cudaFuncAttributes attributes;')
start=s.index('int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {')
cut=s.index('    const int tiles = ',start)
end=s.index('\\nint main(',cut)
setup=s[start:cut]
body=s[cut:end]
body=body.replace('Grid grid(tiles);','Grid grid(tiles, B);')
body=body.replace('initializeMatrix(local, n, grid);','initializeMatrix<B>(local, n, grid);')
body=body.replace('floydWarshall(matrix.data, tiles, grid);','floydWarshall<B>(matrix.data, tiles, grid);')
body=body.replace('gatherResults(local, n, tiles, grid, dist);','gatherResults<B>(local, n, tiles, grid, dist);')
newbody='template<int B>\\nint runDistributedBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {\\n    constexpr int TILE = B;\\n'+body
setup+='    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;\\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));\\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));\\n    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more\\n    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.\\n    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&\\n        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))\\n        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);\\n    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);\\n}\\n'
s=s[:start]+newbody+'\\n'+setup+s[end:]
s=s.replace('value > unsigned(INT_MAX - TILE)','value > unsigned(INT_MAX - 64)')
open(p,'w').write(s)

__device__ __forceinline__ bool relax(Cell& current, Cell left, Cell right,
                                     unsigned int vertex) {
    const unsigned int distance = left.distance + right.distance;
    const unsigned int via = max(vertex, max(left.via, right.via));
    if (distance < current.distance || (distance == current.distance && via < current.via)) {
        current = {distance, via};
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

template<int B>
__global__ void updateDiagonal(Cell* diagonal, unsigned int firstVertex) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;
    constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = B / BLOCK_ROWS;
    __shared__ Cell tile[TILE][TILE];
    const int column = threadIdx.x, firstRow = threadIdx.y;
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        tile[row][column] = diagonal[row * TILE + column];
    }
    __syncthreads();
    #pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            // Zero diagonals and positive edges make pivot rows/columns
            // invariant. Leaving them untouched avoids shared-memory races.
            if (row != k && column != k) {
                Cell value = tile[row][column];
                if (relax(value, tile[row][k], tile[k][column], firstVertex + k + 1))
                    tile[row][column] = value;
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        diagonal[row * TILE + column] = tile[row][column];
    }
}

template<int B>
__global__ void updatePanels(Cell* matrix, const Cell* diagonal, int rows, int columns,
                             int pivotRow, int pivotColumn, bool ownsRow, bool ownsColumn,
                             unsigned int firstVertex) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;
    constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = B / BLOCK_ROWS;
    const bool rowPanel = blockIdx.y == 0;
    const int index = blockIdx.x;
    if (rowPanel ? (!ownsRow || index >= columns || (ownsColumn && index == pivotColumn))
                 : (!ownsColumn || index >= rows || (ownsRow && index == pivotRow))) return;
    const size_t tileIndex = rowPanel ? size_t(pivotRow) * columns + index
                                     : size_t(index) * columns + pivotColumn;
    Cell* output = matrix + tileIndex * TILE_CELLS;
    extern __shared__ Cell shared[];
    Cell (*pivot)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared);
    Cell (*tile)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared + TILE_CELLS);
    const int column = threadIdx.x, firstRow = threadIdx.y;
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        pivot[row][column] = diagonal[row * TILE + column];
        tile[row][column] = output[row * TILE + column];
    }
    __syncthreads();
    #pragma unroll
    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            if (rowPanel ? row != k : column != k) {
                Cell value = tile[row][column];
                const Cell left = rowPanel ? pivot[row][k] : tile[row][k];
                const Cell right = rowPanel ? tile[k][column] : pivot[k][column];
                if (relax(value, left, right, firstVertex + k + 1)) tile[row][column] = value;
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    #pragma unroll
    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
        const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
        output[row * TILE + column] = tile[row][column];
    }
}

template<int B>
__global__ void packColumn(const Cell* matrix, Cell* panel, size_t count,
                           int columns, int pivotColumn) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;
         i += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x)
        panel[i] = matrix[(i / TILE_CELLS * columns + pivotColumn) * TILE_CELLS + i % TILE_CELLS];
}

template<int B>
__global__ void updateRemaining(Cell* matrix, const Cell* columnPanel, const Cell* rowPanel,
                                size_t tiles, int columns, int pivotRow, int pivotColumn,
                                bool ownsRow, bool ownsColumn, unsigned int firstVertex) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;
    constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = B / BLOCK_ROWS;
    extern __shared__ Cell shared[];
    Cell (*left)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared);
    Cell (*right)[TILE] = reinterpret_cast<Cell (*)[TILE]>(shared + TILE_CELLS);
    const int column = threadIdx.x, firstRow = threadIdx.y;
    for (size_t tile = blockIdx.x; tile < tiles; tile += gridDim.x) {
        const int tileRow = int(tile / columns), tileColumn = int(tile % columns);
        if ((ownsRow && tileRow == pivotRow) || (ownsColumn && tileColumn == pivotColumn)) continue;
        Cell values[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            const int element = row * TILE + column;
            left[row][column] = columnPanel[size_t(tileRow) * TILE_CELLS + element];
            right[row][column] = rowPanel[size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_CELLS + element];
            values[r] = matrix[tile * TILE_CELLS + element];
        }
        __syncthreads();
        #pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
            const Cell b = right[k][column];
            #pragma unroll
            for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r)
                relax(values[r], left[firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS][k], b, firstVertex + k + 1);
        }
        #pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
            const int row = firstRow + r * BLOCK_ROWS;
            matrix[tile * TILE_CELLS + row * TILE + column] = values[r];
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
}

template<int B>
__global__ void restorePathIndices(Cell* matrix, size_t count, int columns,
                                   int columnCoordinate, int columnRanks) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;
    for (size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;
         i += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
        const unsigned int column = unsigned(columnCoordinate
            + int(i / TILE_CELLS % columns) * columnRanks) * TILE + i % TILE;
        matrix[i].via = matrix[i].via ? matrix[i].via - 1 : column;
    }
}
unsigned int blocksFor(size_t elements) {
    return unsigned(std::min(size_t(65535), (elements + 255) / 256));
}

// Chunk collectives to support matrices larger than MPI's int count.
void broadcastAsync(Cell* cells, size_t count, int root, MPI_Comm communicator,
                    std::vector<MPI_Request>& requests) {
    auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(cells);
    size_t remaining = count * sizeof(Cell);
    do {
        const int chunk = int(std::min(remaining, size_t(INT_MAX)));
        MPI_Request request;
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Ibcast(bytes, chunk, MPI_BYTE, root, communicator, &request));
        requests.push_back(request);
        bytes += chunk;
        remaining -= chunk;
    } while (remaining);
}
void waitBroadcasts(std::vector<MPI_Request>& requests) {
    if (!requests.empty()) {
        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(int(requests.size()), requests.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));
        requests.clear();
    }
}

bool deviceMPIAvailable(const Grid& grid) {
    int available = 0;
#if defined(OMPI_HAVE_MPI_EXT_CUDA) && OMPI_HAVE_MPI_EXT_CUDA
    available = MPIX_Query_cuda_support();
#endif
    int supported = 0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&available, &supported, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, grid.cart));
    return supported != 0;
}

template<int B>
double floydWarshall(Cell* matrix, int tiles, const Grid& grid) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;
    const bool deviceMPI = deviceMPIAvailable(grid);
    const size_t rowCount = size_t(grid.columns) * TILE_CELLS;
    const size_t columnCount = size_t(grid.rows) * TILE_CELLS;
    DeviceCells diagonal(TILE_CELLS), rowPanel(rowCount), columnPanel(columnCount);
    // Double-buffer GPU receives: MPI can fill the next panel while the
    // previous interior update is still reading the other panel on the GPU.
    DeviceCells nextRow(deviceMPI ? rowCount : 0), nextColumn(deviceMPI ? columnCount : 0);
    PinnedCells hostDiagonal(TILE_CELLS), hostRow(rowCount), hostColumn(columnCount);
    cudaStream_t stream;
    cudaEvent_t copied;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&copied, cudaEventDisableTiming));
    const dim3 threads(TILE, BLOCK_ROWS);
    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests;
    requests.reserve(4);
    // Resolve CUDA's lazy kernel loading before timing the algorithm, just as
    // matrix allocation and graph initialization are outside the original timer.
    if constexpr (B > 32) {
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(updatePanels<B>, cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,
                                        2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell)));
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(updateRemaining<B>, cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,
                                        2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell)));
    }
    cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, updateDiagonal<B>));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, updatePanels<B>));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, packColumn<B>));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, updateRemaining<B>));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, restorePathIndices<B>));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(grid.cart));
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    for (int k = 0; k < tiles; ++k) {
        // Wait only for previous host-to-device copies; the preceding interior
        // update can continue while the CPU progresses communication.
        if (k && grid.size > 1) CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(copied));
        const int ownerRow = k % grid.dims[0], ownerColumn = k % grid.dims[1];
        const bool ownsRow = grid.coords[0] == ownerRow;
        const bool ownsColumn = grid.coords[1] == ownerColumn;
        const bool ownsDiagonal = ownsRow && ownsColumn;
        const int pivotRow = k / grid.dims[0], pivotColumn = k / grid.dims[1];
        Cell* rowData = deviceMPI && (k & 1) ? nextRow.data : rowPanel.data;
        Cell* columnData = deviceMPI && (k & 1) ? nextColumn.data : columnPanel.data;
        Cell* localDiagonal = matrix;
        if (ownsDiagonal) {
            localDiagonal += (size_t(pivotRow) * grid.columns + pivotColumn) * TILE_CELLS;
            updateDiagonal<B><<<1, threads, 0, stream>>>(localDiagonal, k * TILE);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
            if (grid.size > 1) {
                if (!deviceMPI)
                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostDiagonal.data, localDiagonal,
                        TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
            }
        }
        Cell* pivotData = ownsDiagonal ? localDiagonal : diagonal.data;
        Cell* pivotMessage = deviceMPI ? pivotData : hostDiagonal.data;
        if (ownsRow && grid.dims[1] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(pivotMessage, TILE_CELLS, ownerColumn, grid.row, requests);
        if (ownsColumn && grid.dims[0] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(pivotMessage, TILE_CELLS, ownerRow, grid.column, requests);
        waitBroadcasts(requests);
        if ((ownsRow || ownsColumn) && !ownsDiagonal && !deviceMPI)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(diagonal.data, hostDiagonal.data,
                TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
        if ((ownsRow || ownsColumn) && std::max(grid.rows, grid.columns)) {
            updatePanels<B><<<dim3(std::max(grid.rows, grid.columns), 2), threads, 2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), stream>>>(
                matrix, pivotData, grid.rows, grid.columns,
                pivotRow, pivotColumn, ownsRow, ownsColumn, k * TILE);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
        }
        const Cell* localRow = rowData;
        if (ownsRow) localRow = matrix + size_t(pivotRow) * grid.columns * TILE_CELLS;
        if (ownsColumn && columnCount) {
            packColumn<B><<<blocksFor(columnCount), 256, 0, stream>>>(
                matrix, columnData, columnCount, grid.columns, pivotColumn);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
        }
        bool staged = false;
        if (!deviceMPI && ownsRow && grid.dims[0] > 1 && rowCount) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostRow.data, localRow, rowCount * sizeof(Cell),
                cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            staged = true;
        }
        if (!deviceMPI && ownsColumn && grid.dims[1] > 1 && columnCount) {
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostColumn.data, columnData,
                columnCount * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            staged = true;
        }
        if (staged || (deviceMPI && grid.size > 1 && (ownsRow || ownsColumn)))
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        if (grid.dims[0] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(deviceMPI ? const_cast<Cell*>(localRow) : hostRow.data,
                           rowCount, ownerRow, grid.column, requests);
        if (grid.dims[1] > 1)
            broadcastAsync(deviceMPI ? columnData : hostColumn.data,
                           columnCount, ownerColumn, grid.row, requests);
        waitBroadcasts(requests);
        if (!deviceMPI && !ownsRow && rowCount)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rowData, hostRow.data, rowCount * sizeof(Cell),
                cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
        if (!deviceMPI && !ownsColumn && columnCount)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(columnData, hostColumn.data,
                columnCount * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
        if (grid.size > 1) CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(copied, stream));
        const size_t localTiles = size_t(grid.rows) * grid.columns;
        if (grid.rows > int(ownsRow) && grid.columns > int(ownsColumn)) {
            updateRemaining<B><<<unsigned(std::min(localTiles, size_t(65535))), threads, 2 * TILE_CELLS * sizeof(Cell), stream>>>(
                matrix, columnData, localRow, localTiles, grid.columns,
                pivotRow, pivotColumn, ownsRow, ownsColumn, k * TILE);
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
        }
    }
    if (grid.elements) {
        restorePathIndices<B><<<blocksFor(grid.elements), 256, 0, stream>>>(
            matrix, grid.elements, grid.columns, grid.coords[1], grid.dims[1]);
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(copied));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
    return elapsed;
}

template<int B>
void gatherResults(const std::vector<Cell>& local, size_t n, int tiles, const Grid& grid,
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, std::vector<unsigned int>* path = nullptr) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    constexpr int TILE_CELLS = B * B;
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        dist.resize(n * n);
        if (path) path->resize(n * n);
    }
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                const unsigned int a = dist[i * n + k], b = dist[k * n + j];
                if (a < INF && b < INF) invalid |= a + b < dist[i * n + j];
            }
        }
    }
    return !invalid;
}

void printUsage(const char* name) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", name);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
    printf("Launch one MPI rank per GPU; OMP_NUM_THREADS controls host parallelism.\n");
}

template<int B>
int runDistributedBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
    constexpr int TILE = B;
    const int tiles = int((n + TILE - 1) / TILE);
    Grid grid(tiles, B);
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
        printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", n);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
        printf("MPI grid: %d x %d; CUDA tile: %d x %d\n", grid.dims[0], grid.dims[1], TILE, TILE);
        printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    }
    std::vector<Cell> local(grid.elements);
    initializeMatrix<B>(local, n, grid);
    DeviceCells matrix(grid.elements);
    if (grid.elements)
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(matrix.data, local.data(), grid.elements * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    if (grid.rank == 0) printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    const double elapsed = floydWarshall<B>(matrix.data, tiles, grid);
    double seconds = 0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, grid.cart));
    if (grid.rank == 0) {
        printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", static_cast<long long>(seconds * 1000));
        const double operations = double(n) * double(n) * double(n);
        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", seconds > 0 ? operations / seconds / 1e9 : 0.0);
    }
    int status = 0;
    if (printResults || validate) {
        if (grid.elements)
            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(local.data(), matrix.data, grid.elements * sizeof(Cell), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        std::vector<unsigned int> dist;
        gatherResults<B>(local, n, tiles, grid, dist);
        if (grid.rank == 0) {
            if (printResults) print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
            if (validate) {
                printf("Validating result...\n");
                status = validateResult(dist, n) ? 0 : 1;
                printf("Validation: %s\n", status ? "FAILED" : "PASSED");
            }
        }
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, grid.cart));
    return status;
}

int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
    MPI_Comm node;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
    int localRank, devices = 0;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
    if (!devices) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int provided = 0;
    if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided) != MPI_SUCCESS) return 1;
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank));
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_set_errhandler(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERRORS_RETURN));
    if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
        if (worldRank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED support is required.\n");
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false, printResults = false;
    int status = 0;
    bool run = true;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            char* end = nullptr;
            errno = 0;
            const char* argument = argv[++i];
            const unsigned long long value = strtoull(argument, &end, 10);
            if (errno || argument[0] == '-' || end == argument || *end ||
                value > unsigned(INT_MAX - 64) ||
                (value && value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(Cell) / value)) {
                if (worldRank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "Invalid number of nodes: %s\n", argument);
                status = 1;
                run = false;
                break;
            }
            n = size_t(value);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            run = false;
            break;
        } else {
            if (worldRank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            status = 1;
            run = false;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (run) {
        try {
            status = runBenchmark(n, validate, printResults);
        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
            fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", worldRank, error.what());
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Finalize());
    return status;
}
