+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

codex
Larger tiles improved the four-GPU run, so the code now selects 64-wide tiles for larger distributed problems when the GPUs have enough shared memory. Smaller problems use 32-wide tiles. I’ve also added a direct GPU-buffer exchange path for CUDA-aware Open MPI; ordinary MPI uses host staging. CUDA race checking reported no hazards.

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}
+

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

codex
Implemented mandatory MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA parallelism with distributed panels, adaptive GPU tiles, and preserved distance/path semantics. Updated only the existing benchmark files; the executable remains `floydwarshall`.

Exact serial comparisons and CUDA memory/race checks passed on four GPUs. At 16,384 nodes, computation took 1.78 s versus 3.22 s on one GPU. Multi-node transport remains untested.

```bash
cmake -S floydwarshall -B /tmp/fw-build -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release
cmake --build /tmp/fw-build -j
OMP_NUM_THREADS=4 mpirun -np 4 /tmp/fw-build/floydwarshall -n 8192 -v -r
```

+int runBenchmark(size_t n, bool validate, bool printResults) {
+    // Node-local ranks respect CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. A launcher exposing one
+    // GPU per rank naturally selects device zero on each rank.
+    MPI_Comm node;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, worldRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &node));
+    int localRank, devices = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(node, &localRank));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&node));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devices));
+    if (!devices) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: a CUDA device is required.\n", worldRank);
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % devices));
+    int sharedBytes = 0, minimumSharedBytes = 0, ranks = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&sharedBytes, cudaDevAttrMaxSharedMemoryPerBlockOptin, localRank % devices));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&sharedBytes, &minimumSharedBytes, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks));
+    // Wider tiles amortize panel exchange at scale. Small matrices retain more
+    // GPU blocks with 32-wide tiles; GPUs without 64 KiB opt-in use these too.
+    if (minimumSharedBytes >= 2 * 64 * 64 * int(sizeof(Cell)) &&
+        ((ranks > 1 && n >= 1024) || n >= 8192))
+        return runDistributedBenchmark<64>(n, validate, printResults);
+    return runDistributedBenchmark<32>(n, validate, printResults);
+}

     int provided = 0;
     if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided) != MPI_SUCCESS) return 1;

tokens used
150,527
Implemented mandatory MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA parallelism with distributed panels, adaptive GPU tiles, and preserved distance/path semantics. Updated only the existing benchmark files; the executable remains `floydwarshall`.

Exact serial comparisons and CUDA memory/race checks passed on four GPUs. At 16,384 nodes, computation took 1.78 s versus 3.22 s on one GPU. Multi-node transport remains untested.

```bash
cmake -S floydwarshall -B /tmp/fw-build -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release
cmake --build /tmp/fw-build -j
OMP_NUM_THREADS=4 mpirun -np 4 /tmp/fw-build/floydwarshall -n 8192 -v -r
```
